\f2\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 9. Topic-by-Topic Audit Summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\fs28 \cf0 Commercial distinctiveness from theatre acting\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: theatre-style acting feedback may replace Commercial copy/camera/tone evidence.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R01.\u8232 Revision scope note: add Commercial-not-theatre boundary.\u8232 Watch item: keep Acting branch protected.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Commercial distinctiveness from generic screen acting\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: \'93screen naturalism\'94 becomes too generic and ignores copy/product/campaign context.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R01, R02, R04.\u8232 Revision scope note: distinguish Commercial screen evidence from general screen acting.\u8232 Watch item: do not overbuild weak subtypes.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 acting-as-presence/naturalism field semantics\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: 
\f3\fs26 acting
\f0\fs24  score rewards vague presence.\uc0\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R02.\u8232 Revision scope note: clarify 
\f3\fs26 acting
\f0\fs24  as Commercial naturalism, copy communication, camera relationship and tone evidence.\uc0\u8232 Watch item: no score-field or weighting change.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Generic \'93natural / confident / presence\'94 suppression\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: generic praise without evidence.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R03, R20.\u8232 Revision scope note: require observable replacement evidence.\u8232 Watch item: test in output-specificity later.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Tone calibration\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: one-size-fits-all tone advice.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R04.\u8232 Revision scope note: tie tone to supplied brief, copy, product, situation or observable cues.\u8232 Watch item: no invented brand tone in baseline/no-brief mode.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Copy handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: \'93clear delivery\'94 without line, cue-word or message evidence.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R04, R20.\u8232 Revision scope note: add copy evidence anchors.\u8232 Watch item: voiceover evidence is conditional.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Product / brand / situation grounding\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: invented brand, buyer, product or target audience claims.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R04, R05, R17.\u8232 Revision scope note: require supplied or observable grounding.\u8232 Watch item: no-brief outputs must stay cautious.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Direct-to-camera versus reader-scene handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: universal eyeline/camera rules.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R06, R13.\u8232 Revision scope note: split direct-to-camera, off-camera-reader and scene-style handling.\u8232 Watch item: no direct-to-camera default.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 No-dialogue / silent-vignette Commercial\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: non-compliant / missing.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: silent reaction and non-verbal Commercial work ignored.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R07.\u8232 Revision scope note: add conditional task-present handling.\u8232 Watch item: evidence thinner.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Voiceover-style Commercial as conditional\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: voiceover tone lists become all-Commercial categories.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R08, R19.\u8232 Revision scope note: conditional voiceover/spoken-copy only.\u8232 Watch item: do not collapse Commercial into Voice / Singing or VO.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 UGC / social-style limitation\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: unsupported UGC rules invented.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R09.\u8232 Revision scope note: carry as limitation.\u8232 Watch item: no universal UGC rubric.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Presenter-led product limitation\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: presenter/product language overbuilt from weak evidence.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R09.\u8232 Revision scope note: limitation only.\u8232 Watch item: future research only if needed.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Corporate / industrial limitation\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: corporate/industrial assumed from generic Commercial evidence.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R09.\u8232 Revision scope note: limitation only.\u8232 Watch item: do not invent corporate criteria.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 No-brief brand / product / situation invention\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: false specificity.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R05.\u8232 Revision scope note: strengthen no-brief Commercial guardrail.\u8232 Watch item: direct clause limited but baseline rule strong.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Technical assessability versus performance\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: poor tape becomes poor Commercial acting.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R10.\u8232 Revision scope note: technical limitations narrow reliability.\u8232 Watch item: preserve caps and blockers.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Audio / speech audibility\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: poor audio becomes weak copy performance.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R11.\u8232 Revision scope note: audio should describe audibility, not performance merit unless assessable.\u8232 Watch item: no speech/accent deficit inference.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Simple capture / anti-polish\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: studio polish rewarded.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R12, R18.\u8232 Revision scope note: simple capture acceptable if readable.\u8232 Watch item: no class-coded polish.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Equipment / production-value exclusion\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: expensive kit becomes merit.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R12, R18.\u8232 Revision scope note: block equipment as talent proxy.\u8232 Watch item: technical clarity can still matter for assessability.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Reader-as-process support\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: non-compliant / missing.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: paid/basic reader changes talent judgement.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R13.\u8232 Revision scope note: reader response may be observable; reader access is process.\u8232 Watch item: no paid-reader advantage.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Self-tape burden, upload and deadline process boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: non-compliant / missing.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: quick turnaround, upload access or admin treated as ability.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R13, R18.\u8232 Revision scope note: process-only unless explicit brief compliance.\u8232 Watch item: no early-submission merit.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 professional_presentation boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: polish/resource/aesthetic presentation drift.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R18, R12, R17.\u8232 Revision scope note: limit to assessability, brief response and safe process.\u8232 Watch item: preserve category but narrow use.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 brief_adherence / professional standards boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: baseline mode sounds like brief compliance.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R05, R13.\u8232 Revision scope note: explicit no-brief restraint and process-only admin boundaries.\u8232 Watch item: brief mode still allowed where actual brief exists.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Live-room-only / process-only overclaim\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: non-compliant / missing.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: finished tape implies flexibility, improv or direction response.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R14.\u8232 Revision scope note: mark live-room-only unless directly shown.\u8232 Watch item: no callback prediction.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Callback readiness and response-to-direction scope\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: non-compliant / missing.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: unsupported recall/callback prediction.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R14.\u8232 Revision scope note: block or lower confidence.\u8232 Watch item: no live-room inference.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Nudity / stunt / privacy / consent boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: unsafe or inappropriate request treated as professional expectation.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R15.\u8232 Revision scope note: safety/process boundary.\u8232 Watch item: not scoring evidence.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Accessibility-safe and non-deficit handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: access needs or adaptations treated as deficits.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R16.\u8232 Revision scope note: add Commercial-specific non-deficit rules.\u8232 Watch item: accent/speech/gender-diverse voice details remain broad.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Protected-characteristic and stereotype-led suitability boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: stereotype-led \'93fit\'94 or \'93look\'94 comments.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R17.\u8232 Revision scope note: strengthen protected-trait and suitability guardrails.\u8232 Watch item: narrow lawful age advert exceptions only if supplied.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Marketability / bookability / appearance exclusion\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: partial.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: Commercial language drifts into marketability, \'93look\'94, bookability or buyer fit.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R17, R18.\u8232 Revision scope note: explicit exclusion block.\u8232 Watch item: some source evidence inferential, but baseline safety guardrails are strong.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Methodology / practitioner / source-shape overreach\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: non-compliant / missing.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: practitioner shorthand, tone lists or course language become universal rules.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R19.\u8232 Revision scope note: add source-shape caution.\u8232 Watch item: B4 conditional only.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs28 \cf0 Display / label / comparison risks\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current baseline status: not auditable from supplied materials.\uc0\u8232 Key risk: category label mismatch or comparison-page inconsistency.\u8232 Recommendation IDs: R21.\u8232 Revision scope note: carry as watch item; no UI proposal.\u8232 Watch item: requires later output-spec / live QA.\
\pard\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0

\f2\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 17. Reusable Handoff Pack for COMMERCIAL-REV\

\fs28 \cf0 COMMERCIAL-AUDIT handoff summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b0\fs24 \cf0 COMMERCIAL-AUDIT is complete. The current Commercial baseline is directionally correct because Commercial is already an operational type and the supplied baseline recognises clarity, naturalism, camera connection, copy/brand tone and technical readability. However, COMMERCIAL-SYN shows that the baseline is still too generic to be revision-ready. It does not yet strongly prevent Commercial feedback from becoming theatre-style acting feedback or generic screen acting feedback, and it does not yet provide enough observable anchors for the 
\f3\fs26 acting
\f0\fs24  field when it is used as Commercial presence / naturalism.\
The highest-priority revision need is Commercial field semantics. COMMERCIAL-REV should clarify that Commercial acting/presence means observable copy communication, grounded task-calibrated tone, camera relationship, addressee logic, product/situation grounding where supplied and believable scale. Generic language such as \'93natural\'94, \'93confident\'94, \'93strong presence\'94, \'93relatable\'94, \'93authentic\'94, \'93marketable\'94 or \'93bookable\'94 must be replaced with specific behavioural evidence.\
The second priority is claim scope. Commercial reports must not invent brand, product, buyer, target audience, campaign world or casting fit where no brief/copy/product context exists. Direct-to-camera, off-camera-reader and scene-style Commercial work need separate camera and eyeline handling. Voiceover-style evidence is conditional only. UGC/social, presenter-led product and corporate/industrial remain limitations.\
The third priority is fairness and assessability. B2 supports clear sight/sound, task-appropriate framing, controlled eyeline and simple domestic capture as assessability conditions, while B3 blocks production polish, expensive equipment, paid readers, upload/fee burden, protected-characteristic inference, nudity/stunt/privacy overreach and access needs as deficits. Technical or audio weaknesses should narrow reliability before becoming Commercial-performance criticism.\
No score fields, weights, caps, blockers, verdict thresholds, role-fit bounds, backend logic, schema, pipeline or UI changes are supported. COMMERCIAL-REV should create wording, evidence-standard and guardrail revisions only.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0
